                     ___________________________________

                          10. pōliyārāycciyaṉ

   (nūlārāycciyeṉpatu nūliṟ kuṟṟaṅ kūṟutalē yeṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭum, meyyāṉa
   kuṟṟaṅkaḷaik kāṇṭaliṉṟip pōlikkuṟai kūṟikkoṇṭum, nūliṉ nayaṅ kaṇṭu
   nukartaliṉṟi navaikāṇṭalē teḻi
   leṉakkoṇṭum, vāḷā tiritarum oruvaṉaic cuṭṭippāṭiyatu)
   pōli yārāycciyaṉ - False Critic. ip 'pōli yārāycciyaṉ' ṭeṉisaṉ makākavi
   āṅkilattil varaintuḷḷa, The Poet's Mind' eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiṉaip peritun
   taḻuvi eḻuntatu. atu varumāṟu:
   THE POET'S MIND
       (First Published in 1830)
       By
       Alfred Lord Tennyson.
       Vex not thou the poet's mind
       With thy shallow wit:
       Vex not thou the poet's mind:
       For thou canst not fathom it.
       Clear and bright it should be ever,
       Flowing like a crystal river:
       Bright as light, and clear as wind.
       Dark -brow'd sophist, come not anear:
       All the place is holy ground:
       Hollow smile and frozen sneer
       Come not here.
       Holy water will I pour
       Into every spicy flower
       Of the laurel -shrubs that hedge it around.
       The flowers would faint at your cruel cheer.
       In your eye there is death,
       There is frost in your breath
       Which would blight the plants.
       Where you stand you cannot hear
       From the groves within
       The wild -bird's din.
       In the heart of the garden the merry bird chants:
       It would fall to the ground if you came in.
       In the middle leaps a fountain
       Like sheet lighting,
       Ever brightening
       With a low melodious thunder;
       All day and all night it is ever drawn
       From the brain of the purple mountain
       Which stands in the distance yonder:
       It springs on a level of bowery lawn,
       And the mountain draws it from Heaven above,
       And it sings a song of undying love;
       And yet, tho' its voice be so clear and full,
       You never would hear it; your ears are so dull;
       So keep where you are: you are foul with sin;
       It would shrink to the earth if you came in.

   pōliyārāycciyaṉ
   kaliviruttam
       nūlai yuḷḷapaṭi yāyvaleṉu noyyamakaṉē
       pōliyāyapala tōmuraicey puṉmaineṟiyāy
       cīla muḷḷapula vōrmaṉatiṉ cevviyuṇarāy
       vēlai yottaniṉa vīṇmoḻi viṭuttiyoḻiti. (1)
       pānta ḷēpiṟitu pāntaṉiṉa pātamuṇarum
       vāynta mātaṟi makārarumai vantiyuṇarā
       ṉāynta ceypuṇala māṉṟakavi vāṇaraṟivā
       rēynta vūṟuṇaru mōrviḻiya veṅṅaṉaṟivāy? (2)
       matura miṅkoḻuku māṅkaṉi vaṭittacuvaiyār
       mututa miḻkkaviyai muṟṟuṇarki lātupicitat
       tutita ruṅkaṉiyai yottakuṟai yōrumuṟaiyāṟ
       kotikaṇoṉ ṟuṟukoṭuṅkoṭinikarppai koṭiyōy? (3)

   ----------
   1. āyval = ārāycci ceyvēṉ. noyya = aṟpamāṉa. pōliyāya =mey pōlat tōṟṟi
   uḷḷavāṟu nōkkumiṭattup poyyāṉa. tēram = kuṟṟam. uraicey = kūṟum.
   puṉmaineṟi = iḻintavaḻi. cīlam = nalloḻukkam. cevvi = nalliyalpu.
   uṇarāy = aṟiyāy. vēlai yotta = vēlāyutam pōla
   varuttum iyalpuḷḷa. niṉa = niṉṉuṭaiya. vīṇ moḻi = payaṉaṟṟa col.
   viṭutti yoḻivāy = viṭṭoḻivāy. viṭutti =muṟṟeccam.
   2. pāntaḷ = pāmpu. piṟitu = maṟṟoru. pātam = kāl.
   cf. "pāmpiṉ kāl pāmpukkut teriyum,"
   "pulamik kavaraip pulamai terital
       pulamik kavarkkē pulaṉām - nalamikka
       pūmpuṉa lūra potumakkaṭ kākātē
       pāmpaṟiyum pāmpiṉ kāl." - (paḻamoḻi)

   vāynta = peṟṟa. mātu = tāy. makār = kuḻantaikaḷ. vanti = malaṭi.
   cf. "kuḻantaiyarumai tāykkut teriyum."
       "makavarumai malaṭi yaṟiyāḷ."

   āynta = ārāynta. ceyyuḷ nalam = ceyyuḷiṉ aḻaku. āṉṟa = amainta; kalvi
   kēḷvi yamainta eṉṟapaṭi. kavivāṇar = pāvalar. ēynta = poruntiya. ūṟu =
   kuṟṟam. ivaṇ kuṟṟamē yuṇarpavaṉātaliṉ 'ōr viḻiyaṉ' eṉappaṭṭaṉaṉ. ōr
   viḻiyaṉ = orē pārvai (One -sided view) uḷḷavaṉ. āciriyar kuṟippittuḷḷa
   'vāḷā tiritarum pōliyārāycciyaṉām oruva'ṉukkum ēkākṣam pōlum.
   3. cuvaiyār = cuvai poruntiya. picitam = vēmpu. utitarum = uṇṭākum.
   ōrum = ārāyum. kotikaṇ = tīya kaṇ. koṭuṅkoṭi = koṭiya kākam. koṭumai
   vāyntiruppatōṭu koṭi (kākkai)yaiyum nikarppaiyātaliṉ, 'koṭiyōy' eṉṟa
   peyar niṉakkē takum, eṉṟatu kuṟippu. kākkaikkuk kaṇmaṇi yoṉṟu eṉpatu
   kākākṣakōḷa niyāyattālum "kākat tirukaṇṇiṟ koṉṟē maṇikalan
   tāṅkivaruvar," (tirukkōvaiyār -71) eṉpatālum aṟika.
   kaḷina ṟaikkiṉiya vaippuṟaḻ kavīcaṉmaṉamik
       koḷipa ṭaittumaṟu viṉṟuteḷi vuṟṟulavumā
       ṟeḷipa ḷiṅkaṉaiya teḷḷaṟali ṉūṟaleṉalāṉ
       vaḷiye ṉakkulavu vāṉkaṇoḷi māṉumeṉavē. (4)
       kaṇṇi lātuḻal kacaṭṭaṟivi ṉāyaṇukalai
       yeṇṇu mivviṭa melāmiṉiya tūymaiyuṭaiya
       vuṇṇi lāmaleḻu tantatuko luṉṟaṉakaitāṉ
       maṇṇi livviṭaiyiṉ vāralai mayaṅkupoṟiyāy! (5)
       tūya nīrmalarka ṭōṟunaṉi peytuvaruvēṉ
       māyu mammalarkaṇ mākoṭiyai nuṉṟaṉakaiyā
       lēyu niṉviḻiyi leytuṟu miṟappumikavum
       vīyu mūcciṉuṟu veyyapaṉi mēviyavaiyē. (6)

   --------
   4. kaḷi naṟai - kaḷippiṉaiyuṇṭākkum tēṉ. vaippu - vaikkumiṭam. uṟaḻ -
   pōṉṟa; uvamaccol. kavīcaṉ - kaviñarkaḷukkut talaivaṉ. vaippuṟaḻ maṉam
   eṉac cērkka. maṟuviṉṟu - kuṟṟamiṉṟi. teḷivuṟṟu - teḷivuporunti. ulavum
   - cañcarikkum. teḷipaḷiṅku - teḷinta paḷiṅku. (Clear crystal). teḷ
   aṟaliṉ ūṟal - teḷivāṉa nīrūṟṟu. vāṉvaḷi - uyarnta ākāyattilōṭum kāṟṟu.
   ūṟaleṉa vaḷiyeṉa vāṉkaṇ oḷiyeṉa uṟṟulavum eṉa muṭikka. pūmikkuc
   camīpattiṟ cūḻntuḷḷa vāyuvil tūcu poruntiyirukkumēyaṉṟi mēliṭattuḷḷa
   vāyu nirmalamā yirukkumātaliṉ vāṉvaḷi uvamaiyāyiṟṟu.
   vāṉkaṇ - cūriyaṉ. kaṇkaḷ mūṉṟil viṇṇiṟ celluṅkaṇ cūriyaṉē.
   cf."kaṇṇeṉap paṭuva mūṉṟu kāvalaṉ kalvi kāmar,
       viṇṇiṉaic cuḻala vōṭum veyyava ṉeṉṉum pēra." (cūḷāmaṇi - mantira.
       30).
       cūriyaṉoḷiyil aṇukkaḷ oṉṟupaṭṭukkalavā. māṉum - pōlum.

   5. kaṇ ilātu - kaṭpoṟiyiṉṟi. uḻal - uḻalum. kacaṭu - kuṟṟam. aṇu kalai
   - neruṅkalai. tūymaiyuṭaiya - paricuttamāṉavai. uḷnilāmal - maṉattil
   aṭaṅkāmal. eḻutantatu - eḻuntatu. kol - acai. uṉṟaṉ nakai - uṉatu
   ikaḻcci nakai. maṇṇil - ulakil. ivviṭaiyiṉ - iṅku. vāralai - varātē.
   mayaṅku poṟiyāy - uḷḷatai uḷḷavāṟu kāṇun taṉmaiyiṉṟi mayaṅkum kaṇṇākiya
   intiriyattai yuṭaiyavaṉē.
   ic ceyyuḷil 'ivviṭam' eṉṟu kuṟittatu, āciriyar tampāṟ ṟamiḻ payilum
   iyaṟṟamiḻ māṇavarkaṭkup pāṭaṅ kūṟumiṭattiṉai.
   6. tūya - paricuttamāṉa.nīr - nīriṉai. naṉi - mika. mā koṭiyai - mikak
   koṭumai yuḷḷāy. nuṉṟaṉ - niṉatu. ammalarkaḷ nuṉṟaṉ nakaiyāl māyum eṉka.
   ēyum - poruntiya. niṉ viḻiyil - uṉ pārvaiyāl. niṉatu viḻiyāl mikutiyum
   iṟappiṉai aṭaiyum. mūcciṉ uṟuveyya paṉi - uṉatu mūcciluṇṭākum koṭiya
   paṉi. mēvi - porunti. vīyum - aḻiyum. avai veyyapaṉi mēvi līyum eṉka.
   pāṭam payilum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ maṉattai malarākavum, tām pāṭaṅ kūṟalait
   tūya nīr peytalākavum kuṟittuḷḷaṉar.
   cf. "ñāṉa naṉṉīr naṉkaṉan teḷittut
       tēṉā rōti cevimutal vārttu." (maṇimēkalai. 23 -138.)
       maṉṉi vāṉaḷavu kāntamalai mākamuṟumā
       miṉṉu mēkamaṭai viṟkumuṟi vīḻmaḻaiyiṉā
       ṉaṉṉa rōṭaipala nāṟpuṟam viṭuttaparicē
       yeṉṉa vāṉpulava riṉṉuḷa meḻuntoḷirumāl. (7)
       nīyirukkumiṭa niṟṟiyeṉi ṉēcanilavu
       māyi ruñciṉai paṭaittapoḻil vanticaiceyuñ
       cēya viṉkuyilkaḷ pāṭutal cevittoḷaiyuṟāy
       pāya pūmpoḻili ṉāppaṇavai pāṭiyeḻumāl (8)
       kiṭṭi vantaiyeṉi lammavalai kēyamiṉaiyak
       keṭṭa ḻintuviṭu māliṉicai kēṭṭumakiḻaṉ
       maṭṭu maḵkaṟivi ṉiṟkuṟutal vāyvatilaiyā
       ṉaṭṭu nallaṟivu nāḷumuṉai naṇṇukilatē. (9)

   ---------
   7. maṉṉi = nilaipeṟṟu. vāṉ aḷavu = ākāya maḷāviya. kānta malai = kāntak
   kallākiya malai. mākam = viṇ. uṟum = poruntiya. mā miṉṉu mēkam = karu
   niṟattōṭu miṉṉalaic ceyyum mēkam. aṭaivil = muṟaimaiyāka. kumuṟi = iṭi
   yoli ceytu. vīḻ maḻaiyiṉāl = peyyum maḻaiyāl. naṉṉar = cevvitāka. ōṭai
   = nīrōṭai. ōṭai = ai viṉaimutaṟ poruḷ. vikuti. pala ōṭai eṉka. nāṟpuṟam
   viṭutta = nāṉku puṟaṅkaḷilum oḻukkiya. paricē yeṉṉa = vitamē pōla.
   vāṉpulavar = ciṟanta pulamai vāynta pāvalartam.; iṉ uḷam = iṉiya maṉam.
   eḻuntu oḷirum = mēlōṅki viḷaṅkum. kēṭpōr neñcait taṉpā liḻuttup
   piṇikkum vaṉmaiyāl iṉṉuḷa ttiṟkuk kāntamalai yuvamaiyāyiṟṟu.
   8. nī irukkum iṭam niṟṟiyeṉiṉ = nī irukkumiṭattilēyē yiruntuviṭiṉ.
   nēcam nilavum = aṉpu poruntiya. māyirum = mikapperiya. ciṉai =
   kiḷaikaḷ. poḻil = cōlai. icai ceyum = pāṭum. cēya = cevviya. iṉkuyilkaḷ
   = iṉiya kuyiṟpaṟavaikaḷ. pāṭutal = kāṉañceytalai. cevittoḷai uṟāy =
   niṉatu kātiṟkēḷāy. pāya = paranta. pūmpoḻiliṉ nāppaṇ = pūkkaḷ malarnta
   cōlai naṭuvē.
   avai pāṭi eḻum = akkuyilkaḷ ciṟappākap pāṭum.
   'nēca nilavuṅ kuyilkaḷ' eṉavum, 'vanticai ceyuṅ kuyilkaḷ' eṉavum
   taṉittaṉik kūṭṭuka. 'iyaṟṟamiḻ māṇavar' kalvi payiliṭattē yoru
   pūñcōlaiyākavum māṇavarai iṉkuyilkaḷākavuṅ kuṟippittuḷḷār.
   9. kiṭṭivantaiyeṉil = neruṅki vantāyeṉṟāl. amma = irakkak kuṟippu. avai
   = akkuyilkaḷ. kēyam = pāṭṭu. iṉaiya = varunta. keṭṭu aḻintu viṭum = cīr
   kulaintu keṭum. āl = acai. iṉ icai kēṭṭu makiḻal maṭṭum = iṉiya
   kāṉattaik kēṭṭu makiḻcciyaṭaitaṉ māttiram. aḵku aṟiviṉ niṟku = kuṟainta
   aṟiviṉaiyuṭaiya niṉakku. uṟutal = poruntutal. vāyvatu ilai = poruntātu.
   āla = acai. nāḷum nallaṟivu uṉṉai naṭṭu naṇṇukilatē = tiṉamum nalla
   putti uṉṉai virumpi aṭaiyātu. kuṟṟamē yuṇaru miyal puṭaiya niṉakkuk
   kuṇam oru ciṟitum pulaṉākātu.
   cf. "kuṟṟa mēteri vārkuṟu māmuṉi
       coṟṟa pāviṉu mōrkuṟai colvarāl.' (kanta. avai. 6)

   -----------
                     ___________________________________

                            11. ōr aiyappāṭu

   [tām tirukkuṟaḷ nālaṭiyārāti nīti nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟiya ulaka
   nilaiyāmaiyaippaṟṟic cintittuḻit tamakku nikaḻnta tōr aiyappāṭṭiṉai
   yamaittup pāṭiyatu]
   ōr aiyappāṭu
   nilaimaṇṭila vāciriyappā
       kaṭpula ṉillā viṭpulat tōrkura
       ṉēriya peṟṟip pēraṟi vāṉaṟ
       kulakamu mataṉka ṇilakuna yāvaiyu
       nilaiyura vallavōr nikaḻkaṇa mēṉu
       meṉṟu muḻaṅkalun tuṉṟiyō rirappōṉ. (5)

   ------
   aiyappāṭu = cantēkam.
   icceyyuḷ āṅkilattil ṭeṉisaṉ makākavi eḻutiyuḷḷa 'A Voice Spake Out of
   the Skies' eṉṟa kaviyiṉ moḻi peyarppākum. atu varumāṟu:
   "A Voice Spake out of the Skies"
       By
       Alfred Lord Tennyson.
       A voice spoke out of the skies
       To a just man and a wise -
       'The world and all within it
       Will only last a minute!'
       AND A BEGGER BEGAN TO CRY.
       "FOOD, FOOD OR I DIE"!
       IS IT WORTH HIS WHILE TO EAT
       OR MINE TO GIVE HIM MEAT,
       If THE WORLD AND ALL WITHIN IT
       WERE NOTHING THE MEAT MINUTE?

   kaṭpulaṉ illā = kaṇṇukkup pulaṉākāta. viṭpulattu ōr kural = ākāya
   viṭattil oru kural. acarīriyāka ākāyavāṇiyiṉ kural eṉṟapaṭi. nēriya =
   oḻuṅkāṉa. peṟṟi = paṇpu. pēr aṟivāṉaṟku = periya aṟiviṉaiyuṭaiyāṉ
   oruvaṉukku. ulakamum ataṉkaṇ ilakuva yāvaiyum. = inta maṇṇulakamum ataṉ
   kāṇ vaḻum carācarap poruḷkaḷ ellām.
   nikaḻ ōr kaṇamēṉum = ippoḻuti nikaḻum oru kaṇap poḻutēṉum. nilaiyuna
   alla = nilaipeṟṟiruppaṉa alla; aḻintupōm eṉṟapaṭi.
   eṉṟu muḻaṅkalum = eṉṟu muḻaṅkiṉapoḻutu. tuṉṟi = neruṅki. ōr irappōṉ =
   oru piccaikkāraṉ.
   cōṟucō ṟiṉṟēṟ pāṟip paṭuveṉā
       leṉṉāk kūyiṉa ṉitukēṭ ṭaṟiñaṉu
       maṉṉō vuṇavuṇ ṭavaṉivaṇ vāḻtalum
       yāṉavaṟ kiṉṉuṇaviṭutalu mākiya
       vivaṟṟāṟ payaṉeṉai yeytiya vulaku (10)
       mataṉka ṇārporu ḷaṉaittu maṟukaṇat
       taḻiyu mēleṉa vaiyamuṟṟaṉaṉē.

   -------
   cōṟu cōṟu iṉṟēl pāṟippaṭupavaṉ nāṉ - cōṟu pōṭuṅkaḷ cōṟu pōṭuṅkaḷ
   illāviṭiṉ nāṉ paciyāṉ iṟantoḻivēṉ. eṉṉāk kūyiṉaṉ - eṉṟu kūviṉaṉ.
   itu kēṭṭu aṟiñaṉum - immoḻiyaik kēṭṭu avvaṟi vuṭaiyavaṉum. aṉṉō - antō.
   uṇavu uṇṭu - cōṟṟiṉai yuṇṭu. avaṉ ivaṇ vāḻtalum - appiccaikkāraṉ
   ivvulakattil vāḻtalum. yāṉ avaṟku iṉ uṇavu iṭutalum - nāṉ avaṉukku
   iṉiya cōṟu pōṭutalum. ākiya ivaṟṟāl payaṉ
   eṉai - ākiya icceyalukaḷāl yātu payaṉ. eytiya poruntiya. ulakum -
   ivvulakamum. ataṉkaṇ ār poruḷ aṉaittum - ataṉiṭattup poruntiya ellāp
   poruḷkaḷum. maṟu kaṇattu - aṭutta nimiṣattilēyē. aḻiyumēl - aḻintu
   pōkum eṉṟāl. eṉ - eṉṟu. aiyam uṟṟaṉaṉ - cantēkam aṭaintaṉaṉ. ē -
   īṟṟacai.
   ippiccaikkāraṉ cōṟṟaiyuṇṭu pacinīṅki uyirvāḻtalāṉum ivaṉukkuc
   cōṟaḷittup puṇṇiyam perukalāṉum yātu payaṉ uṇṭākum, ivvulakamum
   ataṉpāluḷḷa ellāp poruḷkaḷum maṟukaṇattē aḻintupōmāyiṉ, eṉṟa cantēkamē
   īṇṭuk kuṟitta aiyappāṭākum.
   karṇaṉ iṟakkun taruvāyil taṉpāl vantu iranta antaṇaṉukkut tāṉ atukāṟum
   ceytuḷḷa tarumattaiyellām, atu taṉṉuyirkka araṇāy niṟṟalaiyum
   poruṭpaṭuttātu, tāṉañ ceytiruppatākavum ammakātāṉa palaṉ avaṉaik
   kāttillai; ammākātāṉapalaṉ cittippataṟku muṉṉarē ataṉaiyuñ cērttut
   tāṉañceytal eṅṅaṉam amaivuṭaittākum? eṉṟiṉṉōraṉṉa aiyattai yoruvāṟu
   nikarttatu mēṟkuṟitta aiyappāṭum. ellām nilaipēṟiṉṟi yaḻiyumāyiṉum
   avaṉavaṉ ceyyaṟpāla kaṭamaikaḷaic ceytalē takka teṉpatu āciriyar
   karuttupōlum.
   -----------------
                     ___________________________________

                               12. parinturai.

   (iḵtu iraṭṭaṇait tamiḻp paṇṭitaiyar acalāmpikai yammaiyavarkaḷ
   tamatarumait tantaiyārai yiḻantu maṉaṅkalaṅkiyavaḻi avarkaḷ pāṟparintu
   avarkaḷ āṟṟiyiruttal vēṇṭi nirupameṉa varaintu viṭuttatu.)
   parinturai.
   aṟucīrk kaḻineṭilaṭi āciriyaviruttam.
       iraṭṭaṇaippē rūriṉkaṇ vētiyartaṅ kulamiṉiti ṉilaṅkat tōṉṟi
       maruṭṭuviḻi maṭavārta murimaiyelāṅ kavarntukoṭu mayaṅkip pēcun
       tiruṭṭumoḻi yāḷartamai vātittup puṟaṅkāṭṭic celatto laittut
       teruṭṭuraikaḷ palaveḻutit tikaḻacalām pikaiyammait terivai kāṇka.
       (1)
       vañciviruttam.
       kalainala malkiya kārikaiyī
       ralaikaṭal cūḻtaru mampuviyiṉ
       malaikaḷum vaṉmaiya mānilamum
       nilaikulai tantaḻi nīraṉavē. (2)

   -----------
   1. iraṭṭaṇai - tiṇṭivaṉan ttālukkāvaic cērnta ōrūr. pērūr -perumai
   vāynta ūr. vētiyartaṅ kulam - pirāmaṇakulam. maruṭṭu viḻi - kaṇṭārai
   maruṭṭu miyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya viḻi. iḵtu maṭavārkku aṭai. maṭavārtam urimai
   - mātar cutantaram. kavarntu koṭu - piṭuṅkikkoṇṭu. mayaṅki - kalaṅki,
   aṟiviṉṟi. tiruṭṭu moḻiyāḷar - vañcakappēcciṉar. vātittu - vātañceytu.
   puṟaṅkāṭṭic cela - tōṟṟōṭa. tolaintu - aḻittu. avarkaḷai vātappōril
   veṉṟu eṉṟapaṭi. teruṭṭuraikaḷ - aṟiviṉaip payakkum moḻikaḷ. pala eḻuti
   - palavaṟṟai eḻuti. acalāmpikai; centamiḻp pulamai cevvitiṉ vāynta
   ivvammaiyār iṉiya kaṟpaṉai nalañceṟinta ceyyuṭkaḷai viraiviṉ iyaṟṟum
   āṟṟal paṭaittavar; kēṭṭārp piṇikkuñ colvaṉmai yuṭaiyār. tiruviṭaiyūrt
   talapurāṇam, kānti purāṇam, tilakar māṉmiyam mutaliya pala tamiḻ
   nūlkaḷai iyaṟṟi yiḷḷār. terivai - mātu. centamiḻ terinta cīriya
   nalattāṟ ṟerivai yeṉṉum peyar niṉakkē poruntum eṉpatu kuṟippu.
   2. kalainalam - kalvi yaṟivu. malkiya - nirampiya. kārikai - mātu.
   kārikaiyīr - viḷi. alaikaṭal - alaiyaiyuṭaiya kaṭal; viṉaittokaiyumām.
   ampuvi - pūmi. malaikaḷum - parvataṅkaḷum. vaṉmaiya - valiya. mānilam
   -periya pūmi. nilai kulaitantu - nilaikulaintu; tā - tuṇaiviṉai vikuti.
   aḻinīraṉa - aḻiyun taṉmai yuṭaiyaṉa. pūkampam mutaliya nikaḻuṅ
   kālaṅkaḷil malaikaḷum tannilaimai keṭṭu
   māṟumiyalpiṉavā mātaliṉ, acalam(malai) eṉṉum peyarkkuriya nīyum kalaṅka
   nērvatu ulakiyalpē eṉṟapaṭi.
   kaliviruttam
       ivvuṇmai yaṟintārka ḷetuvariṉuṅ kalaṅkakilā
       rauviyamil kuṇakkuṉṟa yariyakula marapuvaḻāc
       cevviyacīr vāyntirunta tiruppemā ḷaiyaṉuntai
       yavvulakañ ceṉṟamaikēṭ ṭammavuḷa muṭaivarpalar. (3)
       vañciviruttam
       nummōy kaimmai nōṉpukoḷa
       lemmāṇ piṟkaṇ ṭiruppirkolō
       vammā vataṉai yāyvaḻiyuḷ
       vimmā niṟṟal viḷampaṟiyēṉ. (4)
       kaliviruttam.
       eṉṟālum paṇṭitaiyīr rittuyara mivvulaki
       ṉeṉṟālum varaṟpāṟṟeṉ ṟiṉpavuḷaṅ kavaṉṟumikak
       kuṉṟāma lamaintiruttal kōḷeṉṟu nāṭōṟu
       naṉṟāku māṟaṟintu naṭappireṉa nampuvalē. (5)

   ---------
   3. ivvuṇmai yaṟintārkaḷ - ivvuliyalpākiya uṇmaiyait terinta
   aṟivāḷarkaḷ. kalaṅkilār - kalaṅkār. auviyam il - poṟāmaiyillāta.
   kuṇakkuṉṟu - naṟkuṇamākiya kuṉṟu. calippiṉmai, poṟuttal potuttaṉmai.
   ariya - ciṟanta. kulamarapu - kulavoḻukkam. vaḻā - vaḻutavāta,
   tavaṟāta. perumāḷaiyaṉ - acalāmpikai yammaiyiṉ tantai peyar. untai -
   uṉatu tantai.
   avvulakam - mēlulakam. uḷamuṭaital - varuntutal. amma - irakkak
   kuṟippu.
   4. nummōy - niṉatutāy. mōy - tāy. kaimmai nōṉpu - kaṇavaṉai yiḻanta
   makaḷir tamatu uṭampu varunta nōṟkum viratam.
   cf. "aṉparō ṭuṭaṉuṟai vāḻkkaikku
       nōṟṟuṭam paṭuvar." (maṇi. 2. 46.)
       "veḷḷeṭ cāntoṭu puḷippey taṭṭa
       vēḷai ventai valci yākap
       paraṟpey paḷḷip pāyiṉṟu vatiyum
       uyavaṟ peṇṭir." (puṟam. 246)

   ---------
   emmāṇpil - ettakutiyōṭu. āyvaḻi - āyumiṭattu. uḷvimmāniṟṟal - uḷam
   vimmutal, varuntutal. viḷampu - collutal. mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyar.
   5. paṇṭitai - peṇpāl. paṇṭitaiyīr - viḷi. eṉṟuāṉum - eṉṟaikku āyiṉum.
   varal pāṟṟu - varakkūṭiyatu. kavaṉṟu - varunti. kuṉṟāmal - kuṟaiyāmal.
   kōḷ - koḷkai. naṉṟākumāṟu - naṉmaiyuṇṭāmpaṭi. nampuval - nampuvēṉ.
   aṟucīrk kaḻineṭilaṭi yāciriyaviruttam.
       iṉpamē vaṭivākkoṇṭa vemperu māṉṟāṉīṇṭait
       tuṉpamē yevarkkuṅ kāṭṭit tollainal vaḻiyi luyttu
       vaṉpukaḷ palavuñ ceytu vāymaiyait terittup piṉṉar
       naṉperu ñāṉa vīṭu nalkuvaṉ meymmai kaṇṭīr. (6)
       vañciviruttam
       iṉiyi vuṇmai yeyttanī
       riṉiya vuḷḷa mēṅkalīr
       naṉina ṉūlka ṇāṭupu
       maṉiyi runtu vāḻvirē. (7)

   -------
   6. iṉpamē vaṭivākkoṇṭa vempirāṉ - āṉantamayaṉ. īṇṭai - ivvulakattu.
   tollai - paḻamaiyāṉa. uyttu - celutti. vaṉpu - tuṉpam. vāymai - uṇmai.
   terittu - viḷakki. naṉperuñāṉa vīṭu - nalla periya ñāṉamayamāṉa mutti.
   nalkuvaṉ - koṭuppaṉ. meymmai - iḵtuṇmai. kaṇṭīr - muṉṉilaiyacai.
   7. eytta - tuṉapattāl vāṭiya; aṟinta eṉiṉumām. ēṅkalīr - varuntātīr.
   naṉi - mikavum. naṉūlkaḷ - nalla nūlkaḷai. nāṭupu - nāṭi, ārāyntu. maṉi
   - maṉṉi, maṉavorumaiyuṟṟu. vāḻvir - vāḻvīrāka.
   -----------
                     ___________________________________

                 13. muṭicūṭṭiraṭṭai maṇimālai

   [iḵtu ēḻām eṭvarṭ intiyac cakkaravarttiyāka muṭipuṉainta kālattil (1902
   - ākasṭu mātam) eḻutiyatu]
   muṭicūṭṭiraṭṭai maṇimālai.
       collār maṇikkaṭaliṟ ṟōmil vayiramaṇi
       vallāṉ patittaperu māṇpiṟṟāl - nallār
       peruntēvik kiṭṭup piṟaṅku tilakam pōl
       tiruntērko ḷāṅkilanaṟ ṟēm. (1)
       naṟṟē ṉoḻuku naṟumalar tammu ṇalañceṟiyap
       peṟṟē yilarum perumaiko ṭāmarai pīṭuyarum
       aṟṟē yeṉalā maruṅkalai vāṇi yamarntoḷirap
       poṟṟē viyumoruṅ keytiya vāṅkila pūmiyaiyē. (2)

   -----------
   iraṭṭaimaṇimālai: veṇpā, kaṭṭaḷaik kalittuṟaiyākiya iruvakaip pākkaḷāṉ
   vakaikkup pattāka irupatu ceyyuṭkaḷ antātit toṭaiyākap porunta
   amaikkappeṟṟa oruvakaip pirapantam.
   1. col = pukaḻ.ār = nirampiya. maṇikkaṭal - nīlamaṇipōlum niṟamuṭaiya
   kaṭal; navamaṇikaṭkum iruppiṭamākiya kaṭal eṉiṉumām,
   irattiṉākaramātaliṉ. tōmil = kuttam illāta. vayiramaṇi = vaira
   rattiṉam. vallāṉ = toḻil vallavaṉ. māṇpiṟṟu = māṭciyuṭaittu. nallār
   peruntēvikku iṭṭup
   piṟaṅku tilakam = nallārāl pūmātēvikku iṭṭu viḷaṅkum neṟṟippoṭṭu.
   nallār = makaḷir, īṇṭuc cēṭiyar.
   tiruntu = tiruttamāṉa. ēr = aḻaku. tēm = tēyam. nalla āṅkilatēyam eṉka.
   tilakampōl ērkoḷ āṅkila tēyam māṇpittu eṉa muṭikka. maṇik kaṭaṟ
   parappil vairamaṇiyait toḻil vallāṉ patitta māṇpiṉaiyuṭaiyatu āṅkila
   tēyam eṉṟapaṭi. īṇṭuk kūṟiyatu,
   "matiyat taṉṉa vāṇmukam pōlum
       potiyaviḻ tāmaraip putuppūm poykai."

   eṉṟataṉkaṭpōlum uvamaik kuvamai yaṉṟu. tēyamākiya oru poruṭku īruvamai
   kūṟiyateṉka.
   2. naṟumalar = naṟiya maṇamuḷḷa malar. nalam = aḻaku. ceṟiya =
   mikutiyum porunta. ilarum = viḷaṅkum. pīṭu uyarum aṟṟa = perumai
   ciṟakkum attaṉmaittu. eṉalām = eṉṟu kūṟalām. kalaivāṇi = nāmakaḷ.
   poṟṟēvi = poṉṉākiya tēvi, ilakkumi. oruṅku eytiya = cērntu poruntiya.
   āṅkila pūmiyai
   malar tammuḷ tāmarai pīṭu uyarum aṟṟē eṉalām eṉṟu muṭikka.
   cf. "pūviṉuk kaṇikalam poṅku tāmarai." (appar tē. namaccivāyap patikam)
       "pūviṟkut tāmaraiyē poṉṉukkuc carampunatam.". (tiruvaḷḷuva mālai.
       34)
       "pūveṉap paṭuvatu poṟivāḻ pūvē." (nālvar nāṉmaṇimālai. 40.)

   pūmikku ḷōṅkiyavaḷ poṉṉikarcen nāppulavar
       nāmik koḷiru nalamuṭaiyāḷ - ēmak
       kaṭaluṭaiyā ḷāṅkilanaṟ kārikaiyiṟ kammā
       uṭalvayiṟkaṇ pōṉmilaṇṭa ṉūr. (3)
       ūrukku ḷellā muyarntaviv vūriṉi lōṅkuceṅkōl
       pārukku ḷaṅkaṅkuc ciṟṟara cāṇṭār palartamakkuñ
       cīruk keṭuttanaṟ cevviya kāṭṭāc celuttiyavaḷ
       nārukkuriya vijayait tiruppeyar nammaṉaiyē. (4)
       aṉaiyanaṟ ṟāyi ṉarumputalvar tammuṭ
       puṉaiyu muṭikkuriya pūmāṉ - taṉaiyē
       taṉakkunikar koṇṭunikar taṇṇaḷiyuñ cāṉṟa
       maṉakkiṉiya ṉeṭvarṭ makāṉ. (5)
       makāṉeṉṟi yāru makītalat tiṉkaṇ makiḻnturaicīr
       mikāniṉṟa vaḷḷal kalainalaṅ kūṭṭuṇcev vēḷaṉaiyāṉ
       ukāniṉṟa paintē ṉirācamalari ṉoḷirpuyattāṉ
       takāṉeṉ ṟevaruṅ kuṟaicolaṟ killāt takaimaiyaṉē.

   --------
   3. ōṅkiyavaṉ = uyarntavaṉ. poṉ nikar cennāp pulavar = viyāḻaṉai
   nikartta cevviya nāviṉaiyuṭaiya aṟiñar. nā = nāviṉiṭattu. mikku oḷirum
   = mika viḷaṅkum. nalam = naṉmai. ēmam = kāvalākiya; araṇākac cūḻnta
   eṉṟapaṭi. kaṭal uṭaiyāḷ = kaṭaliṉaiyē uṭaiyāka uṭuttavaḷ. iṅkilāntu
   tīvamātaliṉ iṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉar. āṅkila naṟkārikaiyiṟku = iṅkilāntākiya
   nalla peṇṇiṉatu. vēṟṟumai mayakkam. ammā = viyappiṭaic col. uṭalvayiṟ
   kaṇ pōṉm = tēkattiṉiṭattuk kaṇṇiṉaippōla viḷaṅkum. ilaṇṭaṉ ūr -
   ilaṇṭaṉa (London) mānakaram. ilaṇṭaṉūr pōṉ meṉa muṭikka. pōṉm = pōlum
   eṉpataṉ ṟiripu.
   4. ceṅkōl. aracāṭci. ceṅkōlaic celuttiyavaḷ. cīrukku eṭutta = perumai
   mikka. kāṭṭā = tiruṣṭāntamāka. kār = aṉpu. vijayai = Victoria namaṉai =
   namatu tāy. 'ivvūr' eṉṟatu laṇṭaṉ mānakariṉai.
   5.aṉaiya = anta. puṉaiyum muṭikkurira pūmāṉ = muṭiyiṉai aṇitaṟku urimai
   vāynta talaivaṉ. taṉṉaiyē taṉakku nikarkoṇṭu = taṉṉaiyē taṉakku
   nikarākakkoṇṭu; eṉavē oppuyarvaṟṟavaṉ eṉṟapaṭi. nikar = oḷivāynta;
   cf. "nikarmalar nelloṭu tūuyp panaṉmāynta." (cilap. kaṉāt. 2)
       "nīrvār nikarmalar kaṭuppa." (akam. 11)

   taṇṇaḷi = kuḷirnta karuṇai. um = ciṟappummai. cāṉṟa = amainta. maṉakku
   maṉattiṟku. koṇṭu cāṉṟa iṉiyaṉ eṉa muṭikka. makāṉ = periyōṉ.
   6. makītalam = pūmi. cīr mikāniṉṟa = perumai mikukiṉṟa. vaḷḷal =
   varaiyātu koṭuppōṉ. kalainalam = nūlkaḷiṉ cuvai. kūṭṭuṇ = koḷḷai
   koṇṭuṇṇum.
   cf. "kaṭuṅkaṭ kāṉavar kaṭaṟukūṭṭuṇṇum." (perumpā. 116)

   cevvēḷ aṉaiyāṉ = kumārak kaṭavuḷaip pōṉṟavaṉ. paintēṉ ukāniṉṟa = paciya
   tēṉ corikiṉṟa. irāca malar = rōjāpuṣpam. malariṉāl oḷirum puyattāṉeṉka.
   takāṉ = aracāḷat takutiyaṟṟavaṉ. takaiḻaiyaṉ = perumaik kuṇamuṭaiyāṉ.
   maiyaloṉ ṟiṉṟi maṭaleḻutip pāṭunarum
       aiyaṉē kāveṉacco lāṭunarum - tuyya
       maṉattiṉ makiḻciṟantu vāḻttunaru māvār
       iṉattiṉ makaḷi relām. (7)
       elvakai mātaru meṅkaṉ pukaḻcāṉṟa veṭvarṭiṟai
       ulāvarun tōṟumyā mēṟkūṟi yāṅkuḷ ḷurukiniṟpac
       cilāmaya māmeṉa vānant muṟṟut tikaḻntuniṉṟār
       nalāroṭu pollāta mākkaḷu mamma nayantuṉamē. (8)
       mēṉmoḻiyiṟ pānalañcāṉ mēlō reṉaiyarō
       pāṉmaiyoṭaṉ ṉāṉaip parinturaittār - tēṉmoḻiyām
       centamiḻiṉ yāmumavaṉ cīrmaicoli mālaiyoṉ
       ṟuntukaḷip piṟpuṉaintiṭ ṭōm. (9)
       ōmeṉ ṟiṇaṅkiṉark kuṟṟanaṭ pāḷa ṉuḷampiṟitāyt
       tāmeṉṟa kaṉṟu tarukkiṉark kuṟṟa taṉiyariyē
       ṟāmeṉ ṟematiṟai vōṉaip pukaḻi ṉataṉaiyovvōm.
       pōmeṉ ṟikaḻntiṭṭa pōvarkkuñ ceytaṉaṉ poṉṉaruḷē. (10)

   ---------
   7. maiyal = mayakkam. maṭaleḻutal = maṭalūrak karutupavar tām
   virumpiyatalaivaṉ uruvattaic cittirattil eḻutum vaḻakkam īṇṭuk kuṟikkap
   paṭṭuḷḷatu. maṭal eḻutip pāṭutal = ulāmaṭal, vaḷamaṭal mutaliya
   pirapantaṅkaḷai yeḻutip pāṭutal. kā = kāppāyāka. collāṭutal = pēcutal.
   iṉattiṉ = kūṭṭamāṉa.
   8. elāvakai = patumiṉi, caṅkiṉi, cittiṉi, attiṉi, yeṉṉum pala
   vakaippaṭṭa. iṟai = maṉṉaṉ. ulāvarutal = pavaṉivaral. uḷ = maṉam.
   cilāmaya mayam eṉa = nalluruvameṉṟu karuta. paravacamāy niṟpar
   eṉṟapaṭi. nalār = nallavar amma = viyappu. uḷam nayantu niṉṟār eṉka
   9. mēṉmoḻi = mēṉāṭṭu āṅkilamoḻi. pānalam = kavittuvam. cāl = amainta.
   eṉaiyar = ettaṉaiyō peyar. pāṉmai = taṉmai. perumai, aḻaku. aṉṉāṉ =
   eṭvarṭ. parintu = virumpi. uraittār = pukaḻntār. tēṉmoḻiyām = iṉiya
   moḻiyākiya. centamiḻiṉāl = iraṭṭai maṇi mālaiyoṉṟu puṉaintiṭṭōmeṉṟu
   muṭikka. cīrmai = ciṟappu. coli = pukaḻntu. untukaḷippiṉ = mēṉmē leḻuṅ
   kaḷippāl. puṉaital = kaṭṭutal.
   10. ōm = Yes. oppukkōṭaṟ kuṟippu. iṇaṅkiṉar = cammatittaṉar. uṟṟa =
   poruntiya. naṭpāḷaṉ = naṇpaṉ. uḷam piṟitāy = maṉam vēṟupaṭṭu. tām eṉṟu
   = tāmē periyareṉṟu. akaṉṟu = vilaki. tarukkiṉar = akaṅkarittavar. taṉi
   ari ēṟu = oppillāta āṇ ciṅkam. ām = ākum. pukaḻiṉ = pukaḻntāl.
   ataṉai = appukaḻcciyai. ovvōm pōm = oppukkoḷḷōm pōṅkaḷ. pōvar = Boers.
   poṉ aruḷ = aḻakiya karuṇai.
   poṉṉaruḷi ṉūṟṟup pukaḻtōy kuṇakkuṉṟu
       maṉṉuyirppaiṅ kūḻvaḷarkku māveḻili -tuṉṉumaṉpiṉ
       poṅku kaṭaṉītip polivār maṇiviḷakkam
       eṅkaḷiṟai yeṭvarṭeṉ pēm. (11)
       eṉapē cuvateṭvarṭ vaḷḷal kuṇaṅkaḷai yemmuraiyiṉ
       iṉpēr varaiyaṟaik kaṭpaṭu mōvitai yeyttilamāl
       tuṉpē yuḷaṅko ḷiyalpuṭai yāḷarun tuṉṟimakiḻ
       naṉpē rulaka mituvē yeṉaveṇi nāṭuvarē. (11)
       nāṭutaṉic centamiḻi ṉaṟkaṭali lāṭuvīr
       nīṭuvakai koṇṭiṅṅa ṉērmiṉō -kūṭinām
       ellāru meṭvar ṭiṟaivaṉ muṭipuṉaital
       collārap pāṭimakiḻ tum. (13)
       tummiṉuṅ kuṟṟa mirumiṉuṅ kuṟṟañ colippuṭaittār
       nammin tiyaraimuṉ ṉāḷu nalampeṟṟa ñālamaṉṉar
       immaiyiṉ pellā miṉitu nukartan tiruṅkaḷiyā
       ṭemmainikarppavar yārkolip pōḻteṉ ṟirukkutumē. (14.

   ------
   11. ūṟṟu - nirūṟal. pukaḻtōy - pukaḻcērneta. paiṅ kūḻ - payir.
   uyirākiya payir. māeḻili - periya mēkam. tuṉṉum - mikunta. aṉpākiya
   kaṭal. nītiyākiya aḻaku nirampiya māṇikka tīpam. eṅkaḷ iṟai -
   emataracaṉ. eṉpēm - eṉṟu kūṟuvēm.
   12. eṭvarṭ vaḷḷal kuṇaṅkaḷai yeṉ pēcuvatu. iṉpēr - iṉiyapukaḻ. em
   uraiyiṉ varaiyaṟaikkaṇ paṭumō - ematut colliṉ ellaikkaṭ poruntum
   aḷaviṟṟō; mikap peri teṉṟapaṭi.
   itai ittaṉmaiyiṉai. eyttilam - aṟintilēm.
   cf. "muttu ṭaimmulaik kaṇka ṇontaveṉ
       ṟeytta ṭiccilam piraṅkum" (cin. mut. 85)

   tuṉpattaiyē uḷḷattiṟkoḷḷum iyalpiṉar; Pessimists. makiḻtuṉṟi - makiḻcci
   nirampi. naṉpērulakam -tuṟakkam. itu - ivaṉ āḷumnāṭu. nāṭuvar -
   karutuvar.
   13. nāṭu taṉic centamiḻi ṉaṟkaṭal - ārāyappaṭum oppaṟṟa ceviviya
   tamiḻākiya nalla kaṭal. āṭuvīr - mūḻkit tiḷaippīrāka. nīṭu uvakai -
   mikka kaḷippu. nērmiṉ - cērvīr. collāra - vāyāra. makiḻtum - makiḻvōm.
   14. tummiṉāluṅ kuṟṟam irumiṉāluṅ kuṟṟam eṉṟu colli. muṉ - muṟkālattil.
   nalam peṟṟa - ellāc celvaṅkaḷaiyumaṭainta. ñālam - pūmi. ñāla maṉṉar
   nammintiyarai nāḷum puṭaittār eṉak kūṭṭuka.
   immaiyiṉpu - ippiṟappil uḷḷa iṉpam. nukartantu - anupavittu.
   iruṅkaḷiyāṭu - mikka makiḻcciyil mūḻkum. irukkutum - iruppōm.
   irukkumaṟai tāṉmuḻaṅku mintiyanaṟ ṟēyat
       tarukkumaṟṟai yāṅkilarkkuñ cālap - perukkum
       urimaikaḷil vēṟupā ṭoṉṟumuṟā vaṇṇam
       puriyumeṭvarṭ maṉṉaṉ pukaṉṟu. (15)
       maṉṉartam maṉṉeṉṟu vaiyamel lāmoppa vanta veṅkaḷ
       muṉṉava ṉāmeṭvarṭ ṭēḻā vaṉēṉu mutalvaṉeṉṉap
       poṉṉari yātaṉa mēṟi makiḻntu polintaṉṉāl
       naṉṉarik kāṭcikaṇ ṭānanta meytiṉa nāmuḷattē. (16)
       tēvaṉ ṟiruvaruḷāṟ cīrcāṉṟa veṭvarṭeṅ
       kāvalaṉceṅ kōlkoṇṭāṉ kāṇmiṉō -yāvarum
       uḷḷuvantu vāḻttumi ṉōṅkiyakūt tāṭumiṉ
       teḷḷutamiḻ pāṭumiṉ ṟērntu. (17)
       tērntuniṉ ṟīruñ ciṟitaḷa vāyiṉun tēraliruñ
       cērntuniṉ ṟivvayi ṉīrtiṉañ cintai ceyumoruvaṉ
       ārntuniṉ ṟōṅkum paramporu ḷāku mavaṉaiyaṉpu
       kūrntuniṉ ṟeṭvarṭ poruṭṭuk karantoḻal koḷparicē. (19)

   -------
   15. irukku muṟai - irukku vētam. intiya naṟṟēyattarukkum āṅkilarkkum.
   cāla - mika. urimai - pāttiyatai, (Rights). vēṟupāṭu - vittiyācam.
   uṟāvaṇṇam - poruntāvitam. puriyum - ceyyum. naṭuvunilaimai yuṭaiyāṉ
   eṉpatu karutiyatu. pukaṉṟu - virumpi. pukaṉṟu puriyumeṉka.
   cf. "pōreṉiṟ pukalum puṉaikaḻaṉ maṟavar." (puṟam -31.)

   16. maṉṉartam maṉ - aracarkkaracaṉ; Emperor. oppu - oppukkoḷḷa. eṭvar
   ṭēḻāvaṉēṉum mutalvaṉ eṉṉa - ivaṉ ēḻāvatu eṭvarṭāyiruppiṉum mutaṉmai
   vāyntavaṉ eṉṟu collumpaṭi. poṉ ariyātaṉam - poṟciṅkācaṉam. polintaṉaṉ.
   naṉṉar - naṉṟāka. ānantam eytiṉam -
   kaḷippaṭaintōm.
   17. tēvaṉ - kaṭavuḷ. cīrcāṉṟa - ciṟappamainta. eṅkāvalaṉ - ematu
   aracaṉ. yāvirum - nīṅkaḷ ellīrum. uḷ uvantu - maṉaṅkaḷittu. vāḻttumiṉ,
   kūttāṭumiṉ, pāṭumiṉ eṉka. teḷḷutamiḻ - teḷivāṉa tamiḻ. tērntu -
   ārāyntu.
   18. terintavarum teriyātavarum eṉṟapaṭi. tēralir - aṟiyātīr. ivvayiṉ -
   iṅku. nīr tiṉam cintai ceyyum oruvaṉ - nīṅkaḷ tiṉamum tiyāṉikkum
   oppaṟṟa kaṭavuḷ. ārntu - eṅkum niṟaintu. paramporuḷ - mēlāṉa poruḷ.
   aṉpukūrntu - aṉpu mikuntu. karantoḻal - kaikuvit tiṟaiñcutal. koḷ
   paricu - mēṟkoḷḷattakka ceyalākum. eṭvarṭu poruṭṭu kaṭavuḷaik
   karantoḻal mēṟkoḷḷat takkateṉṟu kūṟiyatu.
   cēma cupakirutiṟ ceṅkaṭakac cittiraiyiṟ
       kāmar muṭiyaṇintāṉ kāṇmiṉō - cīmaik
       karacaṉeṉu meṭvar ṭaruñcārva paumap
       paracuperum pēroṭumip pār. (19)
       pāri lavaṉara ceṉṟeṉṟum vāḻka payaṉciṟakka
       cērala rellāru naṭpāḷa rāka ceṟintumukil
       māri poḻikaveṅ keṅkun tamiḻiṉ maṇaṅkamaḻka
       ēriṟ polikana mintiyar vāḻiya veñcolumē. (20)

   -------
   19. cēmam - ṣēmam; naṉmaiyaic ceyyumeṉṟapaṭi. itu cupakirutu
   varuṭattiṟku aṭai. ceṅkaṭakam - cevviya kaṭakamātamākiya āṭittiṅkaḷil.
   cittiraiyil - cittirai nakṣattirattil. kāmar muṭi - aḻakiya kirīṭam.
   cīmaikku - iṅkilāntu nāṭṭiṟku. arum - kiṭaittaṟkariya. cārvapaumaṉ -
   ellāp pūmiyaiyi
   muṭaiyōṉ; piṟaraip paṇiyā tulakāḷpavaṉ; cakkiravartti. ippār - inta
   ulakil. cārvapaump pērōṭum ippāril muṭiyaṇintāṉ eṉa muṭikka. paracu -
   koṇṭāṭap peṟṟa.
   20. pāril - ulakil. avaṉ aracu - avaṉuṭaiya aracāṭci. payaṉ - aṟam
   poruḷ iṉpaṅkaḷākiya payaṉkaḷ. cēralar - cērātavar; pakaivar. ceṟintu -
   tiraṇṭu. mukil - mēkam. māri - maḻai, eṅkeṅkum - evviṭaṅkaḷilum. tamiḻ
   iṉmaṇam - tamiḻiṉ naṟumaṇam. kamaḻka - vīcuka. ēriṟpolika - aḻakiṟ
   ṟikaḻka; iṉi ēr vaḷañ ciṟakka eṉṟatumām. emcolum vāḻiya - ematu kaviyum
   vāḻka.

                     ___________________________________

                         14. paṇṭita nāṭṭam

   [āciriyar, tām irumal nōyāl varuntiya poḻutu, avaratu tantaiyārākiya
   srī kōvinta civaṉārtaṅ kaṭṭaḷaiyaic ciramēṟ koṇṭu tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil
   stalavācamāka nāṟpattaintu nāḷ taṅkiyirunta kālattil, appiṇiyiṉai
   nīkkiyaruḷa ñāṉapaṇṭitaṉākiya tirupparaṅkiri murukaṉaip pāṭiyatu]
   paṇṭita nāṭṭam
   kaṭṭaḷaikkalittuṟai
       ulaka muvappa vuyartami ḻāytanta voṇ pulava
       kalakam viḷaikkum viḻiyā riruvar kaṇavayeḻi
       lilakaṟi vāḷa virumali ṉēṅkā temakkaruḷvāy
       palakalai yōṅkum paraṅkiri meyññāṉa paṇṭitaṉē! (1)
       kāmar cempāṭali yaṟṟupu niṉṉaik karutivantēn
       tēmalar veṭkiyan tāruṭai yāytēva cēṉaimakiḻ
       kōmakaṉēyemai vāṭṭuṅ koṭunōy kumaittaruḷvāy
       pāmayi lāṭum paraṅkiri meyññāṉa paṇṭitaṉē! (2)
       tōmil cempāṭa liyaṟṟupu niṉṉait toḻutuvantē
       nīmakiḻ vuṟṟē mirumaṟ koṭunōyai nīttaruḷvāy
       pūmakaḷ koṅkai yeṉappoli kiṉṟa poruppuṭaiyāy
       pāmaka ḷēttum paraṅkiri meyññāṉa paṇṭitaṉē! (3)

   -----------
   1. ulakam uvappa = cīvāṉmākkaḷ makiḻumpaṭi, nakkīrar peṟṟa
   tiruvaruḷiṉait tāmum peṟakkarutiyē, āciriyar tiru murukāṟṟuppaṭaiyiṉ
   maṅkala mutaṉ moḻiyai īṇṭamaittup pōṟṟiṉar. kūṭaṉmā nakarattuk kulaviya
   tamiḻccaṅkattiṟ kuṉṟa meṟinta kumaravēḷum aṅkattiṉaṉā
   yamarntiruntamaiyiṉ, 'uyartami ḻāytanta koṇpulavā' eṉṟaṉar. āytanta =
   āynta. kalakam viḷaikkum viḻiyār = kalakam viḷaittal viḻikku aṭai.
   iruvar = vaḷḷi, teyvayāṉai eṉṟu mirutēviyar.
   cf. "maṅkaiyar kaṇava mainta rēṟē" (tirumuru. 264)
   eḻil ilaku aṟivāḷa = aḻaku viḷaṅkum aṟivuṭaiyāy. ēṅkātu = tuṉpuṟātu.
   palakalai = palanūlkaḷum. ōṅkum = ciṟantu viḷaṅkum. paṇṭitaṉ (Doctor)
   2. kāmar = aḻaku, viruppam mikka eṉiṉumām. iyaṟṟupu = iyaṟṟi. cempāṭal
   = cevviya pāṭalkaḷai. tēmalar = tēṉporuntiya. veṭci = veṭcit tēmalar
   eṉa māṟuka. tār = mālai. kumaittal = aḻittal. pā mayil = carasvati.
   3. tōm il = kuṟṟamaṟṟa. toḻutu uvantēm = vaṇaṅkik kaḷittēm. makiḻvuṟṟēm
   = kaḷippiṉaiyuṭaiya ematu. nīttaruḷvāy = nīkkiyaruḷvāy. pūmakaḷ koṅkai
   = pūmātēviyiṉ taṉam. polikiṉṟa = viḷaṅkukiṉṟa. poruppu = malai.
   malaikkup pūmātēviyiṉ koṅkaiyai oppiṭuvatu marapu.
   cf "cōtimati vantutavaḻ cōlaimalai yōṭiraṇṭāy
       mētiṉiyāḷ koṅkainikar vēṅkaṭamē" (tiruvēṅkaṭamālai)

   pā makaḷ = carasvati.
   naintuḷam vāṭi yirumali ṉeṟṟuṇṭu nāntaviyā
       tuyntiṭu māṟemai yāṇṭaruḷ ceykuta luṉkaṭaṉāṉ
       maintuṭai yāyeḻiṉ māṇpuṭai yāykalai māṉpukaḻum
       paintamiḻ cāṉṟa paraṅkiri meyññāṉa paṇṭitaṉē! (4)
       nāṇātu nāvalar kūṭṭam pukūuppala nāḷumuṉaip
       pēṇā tiruntilem pēcip pukaḻntaṉem pīṭuyarntāy
       vīṇāka yāmiru malli ṉuḻalal virumputiyō
       pāṇār poḻilkoḷ paraṅkiri meyññāṉa paṇṭitaṉē! (5)
       kaṇṇēyak kaṇṇuṇ maṇiyē maṇiyiṉ katiroḷiyē
       viṇṇēyav viṇṇi ṉiṟaiyē yirumalai vīṭṭiyaru
       ḷeṇṇēyav veṇṇu ḷiruppava ṉētami ḻiṉpulavā
       paṇṇē cuvaiyē paraṅkiri meyññāṉa paṇṭitaṉē! (6)
       kālāḻ kaḷariṟ kaḷiṟeṉa nāyēṅ kacitaraṉiṟ
       kēlātu kāṇṭi yiruntamiḻ vāṇark kiṟaiyavaṉē
       nūlār nayanteri vāye mirumalainūkkiyaruḷ
       pālā kumārā paraṅkiri meyññāṉa paṇṭitaṉē! (7)

   ---------
   4. uḷam naintu - maṉam varunti. eṟṟuṇṭu - aṭipaṭṭu. taviyātu -
   varuntātu. uyntiṭumāṟu - tappip piḻaikkumpaṭi. kaṭaṉ - kaṭamai. āl -
   acai. maintu - valimai. eḻil māṇpu - aḻakiṉ māṭci. kalaimāṉ -
   carasvati. cāṉṟa - amainta.
   5. nāvalar kūṭṭattu nāṇātu pukku eṉac cērkka. pakūu - pukku; pukantu.
   iruntilem - yāmiruntatillai. pukaḻntaṉem - pakaḻntōm. pīṭu - perumai.
   uḻalal - varuntutal. pāṇ - icaippāṭṭu. ār - nirampiya. vaṇṭu kuyil
   mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ icaiyai īṇṭup pāṇ eṉṟaṉar.
   6. viṇ - ākāyam.viṇṇiṉ iṟai - viṇṇulakiṟkut talaivaṉ. eṇ - eṇṇam.
   cuvaiyē - appaṇṇiṉ cuvaiyē. tamiḻiṉ pulavā - iṉiya tamiḻp pulavaṉē.
   7. kaḷar - cēṟṟu nilam. cēṟṟiṟ kālāḻnta yāṉai yeṉṟu collumpaṭi.
   cf. "kālāḻ kaḷariṉ." (tirukkuṟaḷ. 500)
   ēlātu - poruntātu. kacitaral - varuntutal. niṟku - niṉakku. kāṇṭi -
   muṉa ṉilaiyacai. tamiḻ mutaṟ kuravarākiya akattiyar mutaliyōrkku aruḷ
   purinta talaivaṉātaliṉ tamiḻ vāṇark kiṟaiyavaṉē eṉṟaṉar. nūlār nayam -
   nūliluḷḷa aḻaku. nūkkutal - aḻittal. pālā - kuḻantaiyē. kumārā -
   yauvana paruvattai
   yuṭaiyāy.
   kūṭali ṉuṟṟaṉṟu kūṭum pulavar kuḻūuviruntu
       nāṭunak kīra ṉuraiyē yuraiyeṉa nāṭṭiyacēe
       yīṭil vaṇamemai nōyiṉil vāṭṭuta leṉṉaikolō?
       pāṭa ṉalañcāl paraṅkiri meyññāṉa paṇṭitaṉē! (8)
       pārkkuṅ kulattē mirumaliṉ vāṭal parivukolō
       vērkkum pariciṉ viraintaṭi yārkkākkum vīraviṇṇiṉiṉ
       kārkukula mellā moruṅkē tuyiluṟuṅ kāṭciceṟi
       pārkku ḷuyarnta paraṅkiri meyññāṉa paṇṭitaṉē! (9)
       nāvalark keyppiṉil vaippeṉa vivvayi ṉaṇṇiyacīruk
       kāvala ṉēyā mirumaliṉ vāṭiṭak kaiviṭuta
       ṉīviḻai ḻēluṭa ṉēvante nōyiṉai nīkkiyaruḷ
       pāvalark kuṟṟa paraṅkiri meyññāṉa paṇṭitaṉē! (10)
       tuṅkak kuṭammaiyi ṉīṅki virumpit toṭukaṭaliṉ
       maṅkaṟi viṟpukun tōṭi yuḻaṉṟu mayaṅkukiḷḷai
       vaṅkam peṟīi makiḻn teṉṉa veḷiyē makiḻntaṉemāṟ
       paṅkayai vāḻum paraṅkiri meyññāṉa paṇṭitaṉē! (11)

   --------
   8. kūṭaliṉ uṟṟu - maturaiyiṟ cērntu. aṉṟu - muṟkālattil. pulavar -
   caṅkappulavar. kuḻūu - kūṭṭam. nāṭum - ārāyum. kuḻūu - iṉṉicai
   yaḷapeṭai. nakkīraṉ urai - iṟaiyaṉā rakapporuṭ cūttiraṅkaṭku
   nakkīratēvar iyaṟṟiya urai. urai yeṉa - cariyāṉa uraiyeṉṟu. nāṭṭiya -
   nilaipeṟak kūṟiya. murukak
   kaṭavuḷ uruttira caṉma ṉeṉṉum mūṅkaippiḷḷaiyāy avatarittuc caṅkattār
   naṭuviṟ kāraṇikaṉāy vīṟṟiruntu iṟaiyāṉār kūṟiya akapporuṭ cūttiraṅkaṭku
   nakkīraṉārtam muraiyē ciṟantteṉṟu pulappaṭuttiṉār eṉṉum varalāṟu īṇṭuk
   kuṟittatu. cēey - aḷapeṭuttatu viṟiyiṉ poruṭṭu. īṭu il vaṇṇam -
   ittuṉpattiṟku nikar vēṟu tuṉpamillai yeṉṉumpaṭi; valimai keṭupaṭi
   eṉiṉumām. eṉṉai kolō - ēṉō. pāṭal nalam - pāṭaliṉ iṉimai. cāl -
   amainta.
   9. pārkkuṅ kulam - pārppārkulam. vētāntattai neruṅkip pārappavar
   eṉṟapaṭi. kulattēm - kulattiṟ piṟanta yām. parivu - aṉpu. vērkkum vīra
   - tuṉpamuṟṟa aṭiyāraik kāttaṟ poruṭṭut taṉakku viyarvai yuṇṭātalaiyum
   poruṭ paṭuttātu viraintu cellum iyalpuṭaiya vīraṉē eṉṟapaṭi. viṇṇiṉ -
   ākāyap parappiluḷḷa. kārk kulam - mēkakkūṭṭam. oruṅkē - oṉṟākak kūṭi.
   tuyil uṟum - tūṅkum. toṭarpuyarvu niviṟciyaṇi. kāṭciceṟi paraṅkiri
   yeṉka. pār - pūmi. uyarnta - niṉatu paṭai vīṭukaḷiṟ ciṟantatāka
   mutaṉmaiyuṟṟa.
   10. nāvalar - pulavar. eyppiṉil vaippu - cēmaniti. āpattaṉam. ivvayiṉ -
   ivvulakil. naṇṇiya - poruntiya. cīrkkāvalaṉ - ciṟanta kāvalaṉ.
   kaiviṭutal - utavipuriyātu viṭutal; kai upacarukkam. viḻaiyēl -
   virumpātē. kaiviṭutalai viḻaiyēl eṉka. uṭaṉē vantu emnōyiṉai nīkkiyaruḷ
   eṉap
   pirikka. pāvalarkku uṟṟa - pulavarkaṭku aruḷ ceytaṟ poruṭṭup poruntiya.
   11. tuṅkam - uyarvu. kuṭammai - kūṭu. toṭukaṭal - tōṇṭappaṭṭa kaṭal.
   maṅku aṟivil = kuṟainta aṟiviṉāl. uḻaṉṟu - varunti. mayaṅku - kaiyaṟṟa.
   kiḷḷai - kiḷi. vaṅkam - marakkalam. peṟīi - peṟṟu. makiḻnteṉṉa -
   makiḻnta teṉṟu collumpaṭi. eḷiyēm - aṭiyēm. āl - acai. paṅkayai -
   ilakkumi.
   cupam.
